AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='## Root Cause\n\nA **copy-paste defect** in `ExerciseDialog.generateExcercise()`. The Back-position block guarded on the *Angle* flag instead of the *Back* flag:\n\n```java\nboolean isBackActivated = ...BackCheckBox...isChecked();\nInteger backPos = 0;\nif(isAngnleActivated)   // ← BUG: should be isBackActivated\n    backPos = Integer.valueOf(...BackEdit...getText().toString());\n```\n\nSo checking **Angle** made the code parse the **empty `BackEdit`** field, and `Integer.valueOf("")` threw an uncaught `NumberFormatException: For input string: ""`, crashing the app on dialog confirm — matching the report exactly.\n\n## The Fix\n\n**`ExerciseDialog.java`** (the actual bug + hardening):\n1. **Corrected the guard** to `if(isBackActivated)` — the core one-token defect.\n2. **Hardened all numeric parsing** via `readInt()` / `readDouble()` helpers that treat empty/non-numeric text as `0`. The same latent crash existed for *every* box (checking any box and leaving its textbox blank crashed too); now it degrades gracefully instead of throwing.\n3. Added null-safe `isChecked()` / `readText()` view lookups and guards for a null `dialog`/`Schedule` (`Schedule` is left null when `getSchedule()` throws `ScheduleAvailableException`, another latent NPE).\n4. Removed pointless `Integer`/`Double` boxing in favor of primitives.\n\n**`AddExerciseActivity.java`** (fulfills "*and a added Exercise*"):\n- Added an `OnExerciseAddedListener` callback that calls `notifyDataSetChanged()` on the adapter. The refresh call was previously commented out, so a successfully added exercise never appeared in the list. Since the adapter shares the schedule\'s `List` instance, the new exercise now renders immediately.\n\n## Verification\n\n- **Compilation:** `./gradlew` can\'t run here — the sandbox blocks all network access and Android Gradle Plugin 3.1.4 isn\'t in the local cache, so dependency resolution fails before any compilation (an environment limitation, unrelated to the patch). Instead I reconstructed the real compile classpath from the locally cached support/lifecycle/gson artifacts plus `android-28/android.jar` and compiled **all 24 app sources** with `javac -Xlint:all`: **0 errors**, 41 classes emitted.\n- **Behavior:** A test harness reproducing the report\'s steps confirms the old logic throws `NumberFormatException: For input string: ""` while the patched logic returns `angle=45, back=0` without crashing. All 6 scenarios pass, including regression checks (both boxes checked, Back-only, blank input, negative values, junk weight).')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 1396, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 56402, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 1396, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 1, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfYNynGJWJ8eZPgs5Gm6c', stop_reason=None, session_id='de7b5df5-de15-4731-8537-8ddf6e86b90b', uuid='5f5d2032-1ef5-4516-b1cd-b096456ad93a')
